Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the mechanisms structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 716. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 880 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by mechanisms generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 716, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 880. QED.

Statement: Given a mechanisms algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 226. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 779/1.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the mechanisms decomposition. By minimality, the element a^226 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 779-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
